                        </h2>
                        <p class="invisible text36 max-md:text-center font-medium font-sans capitalize w-[25vw] absolute left-[3.5vw] top-[24vw] max-md:static max-md:w-[85%] max-md:mx-auto max-md:mt-6">For centuries, coconuts have carried their own perfectly balanced source of refreshment.</p>
                        <div class="h-screen w-full sticky mt-[10vw] max-md:mt-[12vh] py-[6vw] top-0 flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none overflow-hidden">
                            <div class="rounded-full border-4 border-foreground bg-beige text-foreground aspect-square flex flex-col size-[15vw] max-md:size-[30vw] max-md:border-2 items-center justify-center text-center p-[1vw] max-md:p-2 select-none curved-scroll-box absolute">

                                </div>
                                <p class="text36 max-md:text-[3.2vw] font-inter font-medium leading-tight mt-[-.5vw] max-md:mt-0">Of Potassium</p>
                            </div>
                            <div class="rounded-full border-4 border-beige bg-light-brown text-beige aspect-square flex flex-col size-[15vw] max-md:size-[30vw] max-md:border-2 items-center justify-center text-center p-[1vw] max-md:p-2 select-none curved-scroll-box absolute">
                                <div class="flex font-semibold items-baseline justify-center leading-none font-khand tracking-tighter">

                                </div>
                                <p class="text36 max-md:text-[3.2vw] font-inter font-medium leading-tight mt-[-.5vw] max-md:mt-0">Natural Hydration</p>
                            </div>
                            <div class="rounded-full border-4 border-beige bg-light-brown text-beige aspect-square flex flex-col size-[15vw] max-md:size-[30vw] max-md:border-2 items-center justify-center text-center p-[1vw] max-md:p-2 select-none curved-scroll-box absolute">

            <section id="reviews" class="h-fit relative w-full bg-background z-10 overflow-x-clip pt-[2vw] pb-[8vw] max-md:pt-[6vh] max-md:pb-[10vh]">
                <div class="paddx relative z-10 flex w-full items-center max-md:justify-center justify-between max-md:gap-4">
                    <h2 class="invisible text180 uppercase">Sippers Say !</h2>
                    <div class="flex items-center max-md:hidden gap-[1vw] max-md:gap-3 max-md:shrink-0">
                        <button type="button" class="group relative flex cursor-pointer items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full duration-500 ease-in-out size-[5vw] max-md:size-12 bg-foreground border-2 border-foreground text-background hover:text-foreground" aria-label="Previous review">
                            <span class="pointer-events-none absolute aspect-square rounded-full" style="width:250%;transform:translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0);transition:transform 0.5s ease;background-color:var(--beige)"></span>
                            <span class="relative z-2 flex items-center justify-center">

                <p data-note-item="true" class="relative text150 leading-none" style="font-size:min(3vw, 4.5vh)">No till at the end</p>
                <p data-note-item="true" class="relative text32 max-w-[24vw] max-md:max-w-none font-patrick-hand text-light-beige/70">Palmo is a concept piece — the shop front is real, the shop is not. Nothing was charged and nothing is on its way. Your basket stays exactly where you left it.</p>
                <button data-note-item="true" type="button" class="relative mt-[0.6vw] max-md:mt-2 cursor-pointer rounded-md bg-beige px-[1.6vw] py-[0.7vw] max-md:px-8 max-md:py-3 text36 font-patrick-hand text-foreground hover:bg-light-beige hover:-translate-y-px active:translate-y-0 active:scale-[0.98]">Back to the basket</button>
